3b5(1): sdug pas nan gyis kho mo la nyal po byas nas 'di ltar byas so//de nas slob dpon nor bu bzang pas/'jig rten thams cad kyis blta na sdug pa de'i mthu chen po
3b6(1): shes te/de thog ma btsas ma thag tu rgyal rigs thams cad kyi mtshon rnams shubs nas phyung ste/mda' ste'u ka ma bzhin du thams cad du ltung nas de'i tshe rgyal rigs
3b7(1): thams cad skrag cing rab tu 'jigs par gyur te/'jig rten thams cad kyi blta na sdug pa sems can gyi mchog de'i mthu chen po de lta bu shes nas/de la cung zad bsgo
3b8(1): ba tsam zhig nus te/'jig rten thams can kyis blta na sdug pa de la 'di skad ces smras so //bdag nyid ngan pa bla ma'i chung ma ma rung bar byed pa khyod/nyes pa chen po

16b3(1): par bya//nga ni dpung gnyen mtha' la gnas pa ste/de yang ser 'phreng khyod kyis mi rig go/nga ni yang dag rjogs pa'i sangs rgyas te/sho gam sbyin gyis chos khuzhim po 'thung /
16b4(1): 'dug cing sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyong 'dug cig/tshul khrims btul zhugs yon tan rdzogs pa la 'dug/nga ni yang dang rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas  te//khyod la mtshon rnen sho gam sbyin

17a1(1): @#/ O /rnon sho gam sbyin par bya//ser sna phra dog 'dul mthar nga gnas te//de yang sor 'phreng khyod kyis mi rig go/nga ni yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs
17a2(1): rgyas te//sho gam sbyin gyis chos khu zhim po 'thung //'dug cig sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyod 'dug cig/tshul khrims brtul zhugs yon tan rdzogs la 'dug/nga ni yang dag
17a3(1): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas te//khyod la mtshon rnon sho gam sbyin par bya//skom pa 'dul ba'i mtha' la nga gnas te//de yang song 'phreng khyod kyis mi rigago/nga ni yang dag rdzogs
17a4(1): pa'i sangs rgyas te//sho gam sbyin gyi chos khu zhim po 'thung//'dug cig sor mo'i'aphreng khyod 'dug cig/tshul khrisas brtul zhugs yon tan rjogs la 'dug/nga ni
17a5(1): yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas te//khyod la ('tshan rnon)sho gam sbyin par bya//nga dang nga med mtha' la nga gnas te//de yang sor 'phreng khyodakyis mi rig go/nga ni yangadag rdzogs pa'i sangs
17a6(1): rgyasate//sho gam sbyin gyis cho sa khu zhim po 'thung //'dug cig sor mo'i 'phred ba khyod 'dug cig/tshulakhrims brtul zhugs yon ta na rdzogs la 'dug/nga niyang dag rdzogs
17a7(1): pa'i sangasargyas te//khyod la mtshonarnon sho gam sbyin par bar bya//phung po nges spangs mtha' la nga gnas te/de yang sor 'phreng khyod kyis mi rig go/nga ni yang dag rjogs pa'i
17a8(1): sangs rgyas te//sho gamasbyin gyis chos khu zhim po 'thung //'dug cig sor mo'i 'phring ba khyod 'dug cig/tshul khrims brtul zhugs yon tanardzogs la 'dug/nga ni
17b1(1): yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas te//khyod la mtshon rnon sho gam sbyin par bya//zhi gnas kun gyi mtha' la nga gnas te//de yang sor 'phreng khyod kyis mi rig go/nga ni yang 
17b2(1): dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de//sho gam sbyin gyis chos khu zhim po 'thung //'dug cig sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyod 'dug cig/tshul khrims brtul zhugs yon tan rdzogs la
17b3(1): 'dug/nga ni yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs sargyas te//khyod la mtshon pa rnon sho gam sbyin par ba//lam bcod pa yi mtha' la nga gnas te/de yang sor 'phreng khyod kyas mi rig go/
17b4(1): nga ni yang dag rdzogs pa'i gsangs rgyas te//sho gam gam sbyin gyis chos khu zhem po 'thung //'dug cing sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyo 'dug cig/tshul khrims rtul zhugs
17b5(1): yon tan rdzogs la 'dug/nga ni yang dag rdzos pa'i sangs rgyas te/khyod la mtshon rnaMs sho gam sbyin par bya/stong chen sgo'i mtha' la nga gnas te/do yang sor 'phreng khyod
17b6(1): kyis mi rig go/nga ni yangadag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas te//sho gam sbyin gyis cho khu zhim po 'thung /'dug cig sor mo'i 'phred ba khyod 'dug/tshul khrims
17b7(1): brtul zhugs yon tan rdzos la 'dug/nga ni yang mtshon pa'i sangs rgyas te//khyod la mtshon rnon sho gam sbyin par bya//nyon mongs kun spangs mtha' la
17b8(1): nga gnas te//de yang sor 'phreng khyodakyis mi rig go/nga ni yag dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyasate//sho gam sbyin gyis ches khu zhim po 'thung //'dug cig sor
18a1(1): @#/ O /mo'i 'phreng ba khyod 'dug cig/tshul khrims brtul zhugs yon tan rdzogs la 'dug/nga ni yang dag rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas te//khyod
18a2(1): sa la mtshon rnon sho gam sbyin par bya//sred pa zad pa'i mtha' la nga gnas te//de yang sor 'phreng khyod kyis mi rig go/nga ni yang dag rdzogs pa'i sdangs rgyas te//
18a3(1): sho gam sbyin gyis chos khu zhim po 'thung //'dug cig sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyod 'du ga cig/tshul khrims brtulazhugs yon tan rdzogs la 'dug/nga ni yang dag
18a4(1): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas te/khyod la mtshon rnon sho gam byin par bya//'dod chags bral 'gog mtha' la nga gnas te/de yang sor 'phreng khyod kis mi rig go༐
18a5(1): nga ni yang dag rdzogs pa'i  sangs rgyas te//sho gam sbyin gyis chos khuzhim po 'thung //'dug  cig sor mo'i 'phreng bakhyod 'dug cig/tshul khrimams brtul zhugs
18a6(1): yon tan rdzogs la 'dug /nga ni yang dag rdzogs pa'-i sangs rgyas te//khyod la mtshon rnon shogam sbyin par bya//bde mchog mya ngan 'das mtha' nga gnas te//

18b2(1): 'dug cig sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyod 'dug cig/tshul khrims brtal zhugs yon tan rdzogs la 'dug/nga ni yang dag rdzogs pa'i  sangs rgyas te//mtshon rnon sho gam
18b3(1): khyod la sbyin par bya//shin tu rno ba'i mtshon 'di bor ba la//rig pa'i ye shes skyabs su myur du shog/tshur shog slob dpon ngan 'gro'i dbang song bas//chos ma
18b4(1): yin pa'i phung po chos su sems//de nas bdag nyid khod du chud par gyur//kun kyang chad pas rab 'jigs shing //thams cad la ni srog phangs te//bdag nyid kho na
18b5(1): dper byas la//mi bsad gsod du mi 'jug go/pha rol ji ltar de bzhin bdag/bdag nyid ji lta de bzhin gzhan//bdag gi rang bzhin nyid ni dper byas la//mi bsang
18b6(1): gsod du mi gzhug go/srin po'i byed gzugs de ltar ma byed cig/yan lag lus la rtag tu khrag chags shing //bsad pa'i mtshon cha rnon khrag byugs pa//khyod kyi phrag
18b7(1): la thogs pa mi mdzes so//sor mo'i 'phreng ba mgo las myur du bor//kye ma bram ze'i bu yi las ni 'di 'dra zhig/chos 'dod bram ze'i bu yang 'di 'dra na//srin po ma
18b8(1): rungs pa dag ci 'dra dge/phyugs rnams la yang ma yin mchod yin na//kye ma brtse med sdig grogs dbang song mi//shin tu rno ba'i mtshon cha gdab bsams te//rang 
19a1(1): @#/ O /gi ma yi drung du nye bar 'ong //gang yang sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyod lta bu//sdig byed mi yi nang na gcan gzan yin//srin po'i nang na shin tu sdig can
19a2(1): te||lha min nang na ma rungs la byed pa//sor 'phreng khyod ni shin tu tsher dkris shig/sdig pa sems pa ma yis spangs pa ste//ma ni 'jig rten dag na dga' mchog
19a3(1): byed//nyon mongs zla ba bcu gnyis ram na bsrel//de nas phyir ni btsas par gyur nas kyang //gshang gci mang reg nyon mongs myong bas gsos//bltos shig sor 'phreng 
19a4(1): ba khyod kyi gtso mo ni//mig ni khrag gi mchims rab tu gang //bud med bu la byams shing sdig pa yis//mngar gyi bum pa phrag la thogs te 'ongs//rgan mo nyon mongs
19a5(1): rlung gis skrag yang shig/gos ngan ral ba rlung gis bskyod cing brul//lus la rdul gos rkang pa ser ga byung //rgan mo nyon mongs sdug bsngal chen po myong //lus skams yung ring rlung dang 
19a6(1): lhags pas nyen//sdug bsngal mang pos shin tu gdungs par gyur//bud med de ni rnam par snying nyos te//bsam yas sdug bsngal ldan zhing kyi hud 'bod//de nas sor mo'i 'phreng ba'i
19a7(1): mas||de bzhin gshegs pa dang /bdag gi bu sor mo'i 'phreng ba gnyis phan tshun lan dang yang lan gyis gdab cing cher rtsod pa las/bdag gi bu pham ste lag cha bor bar mthong nas 
19a8(1): sdug pa'i sdug bsngal chen pos reg ste/bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di gsol to//bcom ldan rin chen gter stor ba//bdag gis nyon mongs myong bar rnyed//
19b1(1): bdag gi mig tu gyur pa thob//mig gi rdul rnams bdag gis gsal//bdag gi bu ni mi khrig gis//kye ma lus dang zas la reg/kun tu 'khyam zhing mtshon rnon thogs//
19b2(1): 'gro ba mang po'i phung po gsod//bdag gi bu yang mgu bya zhing //sangs rgyas phyag bya 'am ji ltar bya//khyod kyi bu ngan 'di 'dra zhes//'gro mang tshig rtsub mnyan mi nus//de
19b3(1): la sogs pa rnam pa du mar smra'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis sor mo'i 'phreng ba la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyod kyi gtso mo ni shing drung na 'dug
19b4(1): ste;de lta bu shin tu dga' ba byed pa'i bud med 'di la khyod ral gri thogs te/bdag gi ma mtho ris su 'gro bar 'gyur ro snyam nas bsad par 'dod pa ni/sor mo'i 'phreng ba chos
19b5(1): ma yin pa'i phung po la chos su nor to//'di lta ste dper na/sos ka dbyar zla ra ba'i dus la smig rgyu ba la chu yin snyam nas/ri dgas rnams kun tu byam pa de bzhin du/sor mo'i
19b6(1): 'phreng ba khyod kyang /slob dpon ngan pa'i man ngag gis rmongs par gyur te/chos ma yin pa la chos su sems pa'i sems can ni/mnar med pa zhes bya ba'i mi bzad pa chen por
19b7(1): 'gro'o//sor mo'i 'phreng ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i skyabs su myur du song cig song cig/zhugs shig sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyodam 'jigs shig/de bzhin gshegs pa ni 'jig+sa
19b8(1): pa med pa'i 'byung gnas te/sangs rgyas ni sems can thams cad la sgra gcan zin dang /mtshungs par gzigs pa/na ba rnams gso ba skyabs med pa rnams kyi skyabs so//
20a1(1): @#//sangs rgyas ni dben nas na bzhugs pa/rtsa lag med pa rnams kyi rtsa lag go/sangs rgyas ni dbul po rnams kyi mi zad pa'i gter ro//sangs rgyas
20a2(1): ni lam ston pa rnams la/lam bzang po ston pa'o//sangs rgyas ni 'jigs pas gzir ba rnams kyi skyabs su gyur pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa ni gru zhig pa rnams kyi
20a3(1): grur gyur pa'o//mtshon rnon po myur du bor la/rab tu byung ba dang bsnyen par rdzogs pa long zhig song la bla ma'i rkang pa la btugs te/bzod pa gsol cig/rgan
20a4(1): mo nyon mongs ma la rab tu byung bar grong zhig ces gsol ba thob la/srid pa mi zad pa las sems can rnams sgrol cig dang /sho gam bzang po rab tu byung ba dang /
20a5(1): bsnyen par rdzogs pa sbyin no//sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyod yun ring por lam ngan par 'khyams shing /ngal bar gyur gyis/chos kyi re'i chu 'thung zhing /de bzhin
20a6(1): gshegs pa yang len pa yin te/sor mo'i 'phreng ba de bzhin gshegs pa ni sems can thams cad kyi sho gam len pa lam sel gyi rgyal po yin te/sems can
20a7(1): thams cad srid pa'i rgya mtsho las bsgral zhing lam sel rtag tu sho gam len to//de nas sor mo'i 'phreng bas de nyid du srin po ltar shin tu rno zhing mi bzad pa'i mtshon

20b2(1): phud nas kyi hud zhes zer te/dper na mi zhig nyal ba las rang gi rkang pa la sbrul gyis zin ma thag tu myur du sad nas/lag pa bskums pa las sprugs te/
20b3(1): shin tu mi bzad pa'i sbrul bzung nas 'dor ba de bzhin du sor mo'i 'phreng ba yang rang gi mgo las sor mo'i 'phreng ba myur du bor ro//ji ltar bdag nyid gdon dang 
20b4(1): bral bar shes nas ngo tsha ba de bzhin du de nas sor mo'i 'phreng bas kyang bdag nyid mngon par rig nas/me tog rtsa lag mtsho zhes bya ba'i kha dog ltar khrag gis lus
20b5(1): thams cad yog cing //sprin chen po ltar mig mchi mas gang nas rab tu du zhing bdag la brdeg te/ji ltar sbrul gyis zin nas smyos pa'i mi sbrul ltar ltos 'phye zhing 'gro 
20b6(1): ba de bzhin du/sor mo'i 'phreng ba yang tshad shing sum cu rtsa dgu kho ra 'khor yug gi sa la ltos 'phre zhing song nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs (pha)mgo bo la bzhag ste/

20b8(1): shes chu//rgya mtsho che las bdag deng nges par bsgral//kye ma sangs rgyas thugs rje mchog mnga' zhing //skye 'dul kha bsgyur bdag gi slod gshegs pas//shing rtsa
21a1(1): @#/ O /'bras bu lo ma ma mang po yi//dgon pa las ni deng bdag nges par bsgral//kye ma sangs rgyas dga' ba mchog mnga' zhing //skyes 'dul kha

21a5(1): skyabs mdzad gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis sor mo'i 'phreng ba la slar bka' stsal pa/sor mo'i 'phreng ba khyod longs la myur du song ste/a ma'i rkang pa la

21b1(1): bdag gis mi bzad bgyis//bdag gis sdig phung mang po rnyed//ma khyed bdag gi rten lags te//sangs rgyas shin tu skyabs su dga'//khyod kyi zhabs ni mgor bzhag ste//
21b2(1): rang byung bsnyen par rdzogs par gsol//de nas mas rang gi bu la tshigs su bcad pas smras pa//ngas ni bu khyod btang zin gyi//rang byung 'jig rten pha rol spyod//bdag kyang 
21b3(1): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la//de bzhin rab tu byung bar gsol//kye ma bcom ldan bsam mi khyab//de bzhin gshegs pa lha yi lha//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis rgan mo
21b4(1): la tshigs su bcad pa 'di skad gsungs so//khyod kyi bu ni nga mdun du//rab tu byung bar gnas pa ni//legs so bud med nyon mongs ma//rgyun mi 'chad par bde bar shog/
21b5(1): khyod ni rab tu 'byung ba yi//dus las shin tu yol zin gyis//dge bsnyen ma yi chos rnams kyi//rgan mo bdag nyid dbugs phyung shig/skad cig tsam du 'dir sdod de//rgyal
21b6(1): po gsal rgyal ji srid na//'di dag du yang mthong 'gyur ba//de tsam du yang bde bar 'dug/de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i lha rnams/lha'i bu mo'i tshogs dang 
21b7(1): bcas pas bskor cing mdun du bdar te/snang ba chen pos mnyan du yod pa'i yul thams cad snang bar byas nas/de bzhin gshegs pa dang sor mo'i 'phreng ba gnyis mi ltar 'thab
21b8(1): pa dang /cang shes pa ltar 'thab pa mthong ste/rab tu dga' nas/bcom ldan 'das dang /sor mo'i 'phreng ba gnyis la tshigs su bcad pa 'di skad smras so//kye ma mi
22a1(1): @#//'dul kha lo bsgyur//sangs rgyas rol pa bzang po mdzes//kye ma 'di ltar sangs rgyas kyi//sor mo'i 'phreng ba rab tu 'dul//rtag tu mi yi khrag
22a2(1): gis lus byugs te//lha min dbang dang lha min srin po dang //gnod sbyin stobs dang ldan pa rtsal chen dang //klu dang sha za lto 'phye mi 'am ci//stobs ldan rtsal chen nam mkha' lding 
22a3(1): dag dang //sems can gtam po stobs ldan gzhan dag kyang //sor 'phreng ming thos nye bzhin mig btsums 'dug/de 'dra gtum po'i las byed sor 'phreng de//mi yi rgyal po
22a4(1): tsam gyis ga la gsod//de ni btsas ma thag tu de'i tshe//lha dang lha ma yin pa kun skrag na//mi yi rgyal po tsam la lta ci smos//ming go cha mtshon kun rdol cing snang //de ltar drag
22a5(1): cing stobs ldan rtsal che ba//kye ma de bzhin gshegs kyis btul ba legs//mthu dang rtsal kyang bsam gyis mi khyab mnga'//sangs rgyas bsam gyis mi khyab sangs rgyas ni//
22a6(1): yul dang ye shes spyod yul bsam mi khyab//sor mo'i 'phreng ba de ring mdzes par snang //shin tu gzugs bzang gtso mo'i gser ltar gyur//nyi ma bzod shar kha dog lta bur mdzes/
22a7(1): sor 'phreng byad sdug tshul khrims gnas pa mdzes//tshul khrims bral bas mi ni yongs mi mdzes//kye ma bdag deng khyim chos mang thos snyam//sor mo'i 'phreng ba chos gos
22a8(1): gsol bar gyi//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la thugs brtse bzhes//sor mo'i 'phreng ba dge sbyong (tshul 'dra stsal//slong ba kun gyi rgyal po chen po la//de bzhin gshegs pa'i
22b1(1): legs par gzigs su gsol//de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis/sor mo'i 'phreng ba la 'di skad ces smras so//kye (ba)skyes bu dam pa nga'i lha'i gos mchog 'di khyod kyis chos( )
22b2(1): chos gos gyis shig/de nas sor mo'i 'phreng bas/lha'i dbang po brgya byin la 'di skad ces smras so//sbrang ma lta bu khyod su zhig brgya byin ma dad pa'i sbyin pa nga len tam/brgya
22b3(1): byin khyod ni 'dod pa'i bong bu ste//khyod kyis da dung chu klung ma rgal te//rang bzhin gyis gcer bus gzhan la ji ltar gos sbyin/khyod ni rang bzhin gyis gcer bu na chos gos kyi
22b4(1): phyir ji ltar rin thang med pa'i gos gzhan la sbyin/dper na rgyal po'i gyad stong thub/bdag nyid lus thams cad thog mar 'gyel nas/ba lang glang po 'dam du song ba bzhin
22b5(1): ma rgal ba nyid du 'gyel bas/rgyal po gzhan gyi gyad stong thub pa la ji ltar brgal bar bya/de bzhin du ngas kyang rin thang med pa'i gos gyon na/brgya byin mig stong can nyon mongs pa'i
22b6(1): bdud bye ba dang /rang bzhin gyi bdud la sogs pa dang /ji ltar 'gyed ngas ni bdud tha yas pa dang 'thab cing /nyon mongs pa mang po zad par byas te/dge sbyong gi chos de bzhin
22b7(1): gshegs pa yi bsngags pa'i sbyangs pa'i yon tan bcu gsum la spyad par bya yis/khyod kyi mal cha ni de ltar gnas par mi bya'o//brgya byin mig stong can( ) khyod ni lha rnams kyi
22b8(1): dbang phyug ma yin te/khyod ni dmus long du gyur pa dang khyad par med do//brgya byin khyod kyis gtum po'i lam gyi sdig pa ci 'dra ba'i bye brag mi shes so//sor mo'i 'phreng ba
23a1(1): @#/ O /gtum po yin pa sbrang bu khyod kyis ji ltar shes/kye ma brgya byin gyis gsal bar gtum po'i las mi shes so//bstan pa'i tshul shes na
23a2(1): rab tu 'byung bar 'dod pa thog mar rin thang med pa'i chos gos gon nas/dge sbyong ci 'dra ba zhig tu 'gyur/brgya byin khyod ni rab tu 'byung ba dben pa tsam yang (ma) shes
23a3(1): te/kye ma brgya byin khyod ni bstan pa las phyi rol tu gyur pa'o//'di lta ste nor bu dang mu tig bai DUr+ya dang gser dang nor bu zla mdzes la sogs pa rin po ches gang ba'i bum
23a4(1): pa brgyad khri dang sa le sgram dang /rin po che'i bum pa gzhan yang dpag tu med pa spangs te/mchil ma'i thal ba bzhin du rin thang med pa'i gos bor te gang dge sbyong gi
23a5(1): chos la nges par 'byung ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sras kyi thu bo yang gnas brtan 'od srung chen po gnas mal nags khrod du mcha' zhing sbyangs pa'i yon tan bcu

23b1(1): par mi 'gyur te/bde bar gyur cig ces brtan pa'i phyir 'byung la/bum pa re res kyang ma 'ongs pa'i dge sbyong rnams nam mtsho'i bar du za ba dang bca' ba dang /ldag pa dang

23b4(1): bran dang bran mo dang nor rnams pa mang po dang zhing gi dngos po dang /de bzhin du nyo ba dang /'tshong pa de ni dge sbyong gi chos dang 'thun pa ma yin no//de dag ni khyim na gnas pa

38b1(1): byi lar gyur pa'i sems can gang rtag tu sha za ba la chags shing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po la yid mi ches pa'i sems can de dag ni da dung yang sha la chags shing srog bcod pa

38b3(1): gshegs pa'i snying po las phyir phyogs pa de dag ni gzhan ma yin te/gzhan gyi srog bcod cing  sha za ba byi la'i gzugs su gyur ba'i srin po'o//gang byi ba dang ma ning du gyur
38b4(1): pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i snyingi po las phyir phyogs pa'i sems can de dag na da dung yang chang 'thung ba'i byi ba dang ma ning du gyur te/shin tu mi gtsang ba 'thung ba yin no//ma 'ongs

52b3(1): rig par bya'o//smras pa/nad pa lta bu tshig rnam pa gsum ste/nad pa kha cig sman pa rnyed na 'tsho ba yod do//nad pa kha cig sman ma rnyed kyang 'tsho ba yod do//nad ba kha

52b7(1): pa bya ba shes pa ma yin gyis/zas mi bza' ba'i chos dang btang ba yang shes so//gal te thams cad mi shes na dus ma yin par 'chi bas shi bar 'gyur ro//de'i
52b8(1): phyir nad pa gsor rung ba dang /gsor mi rung pa ste/sman pa yang rnyed sman dang /g.yog rnyed kyang /gal te 'chi na ni de don med pa'i gnas su 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir
53a1(1): @#/ O /zhe na;gal te sman dang g.yog dang sman pa yang ma rnyed/sman pa rnyed la sman ma rnyed pa ste/de ci'i phyir sman pa dang sman rnyed kyang mi 'tsho 
53a2(1): sana|sman pa dang /sman gi bye brag kyang med do//de'i phyir tshe'i rigs byed las lus lci ba dang /yang ba'i phyir bya ba thams cad 'byung ste/de'i phyir btsun pa mo'u
53a3(1): 'gal gyi bu chen po gsor rung yang rung la/mi rung yang mi rung zhes bya bar rnam pa gsum du rtog pa rnam gsum med do//de ni nyan thos kyi shes pa ste/sbrang bu lta bu'i 
53a4(1): shes pa'o zhes makhas pa rnams 'dzer te/mi mkhas pa rnams kyi 'phyas rnam pa gnyis su rtag ste/de la log pa nges pa zhes bya ba ni 'dod pa chen po'o//yang dag
53a5(1): par nges pa zhes bya ba ni nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas so//btsun pa mo'u 'gal gyi bu ngo mtshar rnam pa gnyis te/rnam pa gnyis gang zhe na/de bzhin gshegs
53a6(1): pa ni mchog gi gnas su ngo mtshar du gyur pa yin no//de bzhin gshegs pa las mtho ba'i gnas gzhan mi snang ste ngo mtshar to//dam pa'i gnas su yang 'dod mangs las
53a7(1): gzhan mi snang ngo //dper na de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyan thos rnams/pha rol tu phyin pa bcu yongs su rdzogs par byed cing 'gro ba de bzhin du/'dod chen pa'ang pha rol

53b2(1): 'ang nam yi dgas kyi 'jig rten du song ba de'i tshe/der g.yem pa'i sems skye ste/de'i tshe myur du bud med mang po byung ste/bu mang po yang byung ste/sems tsam gyis bde

53b4(1): bu rnams byin pa de dag kyang za'o//yang bdag nyid kyang thams cad la yang za ste/de ltar 'dod chen gyi sbyin pa'i pho rol tu phyin pa rdzogs par 'gyur te/de bzhin du

53b6(1): chen pa rnams so//btsun pa mo'u 'gal gyi bu de ltar ngo mtshar rnam pa gnyis te/log par nges pa'i gang zag ni 'dod chen pa yin par soms shig/yang dag
53b7(1): par nges pa'i gang zag ni de bzhin gshegs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i nyan thos yin par soms shig/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis sor mo'i 'phreng ba

54b1(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das cang mi gsung bar gyur pa dang /sor mo'i 'phreng bas bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das gang 'jig rten na
54b2(1): skyabs gcig po don dam pa'i skyabs su ma 'tshal de dag thabs gnyis po la bkod de/de gnyis 'jig rten na skyabs lags te/gzhon nur gyur nas bsnyen
54b3(1): par rdzogs pa mi gsal de dge sbyong lags sam/gang skyabs gcig po ma 'tshal ba de dag skyabs sbyor bar lta ga la 'gyur/gang bcom ldan 'das la don dam pa'i
54b4(1): skyabs su ma 'tshal de dag/thog ma nas skyabs su 'gro ba'ang ma sbyangs na dge sbyong du lta ga la 'gyur/de ltar skyabs su mchi ba yang gsal bar ma 'tshal na/sbyin

55a2(1): shig|bcom ldan 'das la sor mo'i 'phreng bas 'di skad ces gsol to//bdag ni brdzun smra ba spangs pa'i tshul khrim mi 'dzin to//chos thams cad ni mi bden pa

55b1(1): ni bden pa ste/ji tsam du thabs kyi bya ba dang /'jig rten na thabs kyis bya ba la bdag yongs su sbyong ba'i bar du brdzun smra ba'i tshul khrims mi sbyong ngo //de kho na'i chos bden pa'i

58b8(1): lag lag bdun zhes nyan thos kyi theg pa la grags pa'i tshig ste/theg pa chen po la bdun po de dag/u dum wa ra'i me tog rgyas pa bzhin du rnyed par dka' ba'i tshig
59a1(1): @#//byang chub kyi yan lag bdun po de dag ste/de bzhin gshegs pa rtag pa'i shin tu me tog rgyas pa bdun no//ci brgyad ce na/'phags pa'i
59a2(1): sa lam yan lag brgyad ces nyan thos kyi theg pa la grags pa'i tshig ste/theg pa chen po las kyang 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad po yang dag par lta ba la sogs
59a3(1): pa'o//gzhan yang de bzhin gshegs pa rtag pa mchog go zhes rab tu ston pa ni/'phags pa'i lam yan lagabargyad pa yin te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i che ba thos shing rnam
